Overfitting: the model is too complex for the available data: it fits the random noise of the training set in addition to the underlying pattern, so it does not generalize. The training error is very low, while the error on unseen data is much higher.

As model complexity grows, the training error keeps decreasing, whereas the error on unseen data first decreases and then increases. The best complexity is at the minimum of the error on unseen (validation) data.

Проверяют три вещи: в каждом определении названы обе ошибки; в определении overfitting есть слово noise; сказано, что диагноз требует данных, не участвовавших в обучении. Ответ «overfitting — когда ошибка на тесте большая» — половина балла: то же верно для underfitting. Формулировки профессора (лекция 3, слайд 3) стоит помнить дословно: "Too simple models Underfit the data ..." и "Too complex models Overfit the data and do not Generalize".

Урок 17 · Бритва Оккама · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

State the inductive hypothesis on which learning from examples relies. Under which conditions can it be trusted, and what makes it fail?

Show model answer

A solution approximating the target function over a sufficiently large set of training examples will also approximate it over unobserved examples. In terms of errors: with enough training examples, a small training error implies a small expected (true) error, \(\hat E_{\text{train}} \approx E\).

It can be trusted when (i) training examples and unobserved examples are i.i.d. samples from the same distribution, (ii) the training set is sufficiently large compared with the complexity of the model, and (iii) the model captures the underlying structure rather than the noise.

It fails under distribution shift, with a small dataset, and with a model that is too complex for the data. The weights are chosen to minimize the error on those very training points, so the training error is an optimistic estimate of the true error, and the gap grows with model complexity: overfitting.

Баллы — за само утверждение и за условия. Первая фраза ответа — дословно со слайда (лекция 3, слайд 3); её опорные слова — target function, sufficiently large set, unobserved examples. Не называй это inductive bias, как делает конспект: это другое понятие — предпочтения самого алгоритма, например бритва Оккама.

Урок 17 · Бритва Оккама · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A feed-forward neural network with one hidden layer is a universal approximator. Explain why this does not guarantee good generalization, and what Ockham's razor suggests.

Show model answer

The theorem is an existence statement about what the network can represent: with enough hidden units it can approximate any continuous (more generally, measurable) function on a compact set to any desired degree of accuracy (Hornik, 1991). A single layer can do it, but it doesn't mean we can find the necessary weights, an exponential number of hidden units may be required, and it might be useless in practice if it does not generalize. The theorem says nothing about what is learned from a finite, noisy training set.

A model that can represent anything can also represent the noise in the training targets, so a very low training error can be reached without learning the underlying function: overfitting.

Ockham's razor, "Entia non sunt multiplicanda praeter necessitatem" (entities must not be multiplied beyond necessity): among the models that explain the training data, prefer the least complex one that still generalizes. In practice: select the model on held-out data, limit the number of units, or regularize (weight decay, early stopping, dropout).

Экзаменатор ждёт противопоставления «represent — learn» и слова noise. Частая ошибка — пересказать теорему и остановиться: вопрос не о ней, а о том, чего она не обещает. Первая половина вопроса — это третье «but» слайда (лекция 3, слайд 2): "… but it might be useless in practice if it does not generalize!"

Урок 17 · Бритва Оккама · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

At a query point \(x_0\) the true function value is \(f(x_0) = 2.0\) and the noise variance is \(0.25\). The same model is trained on three different training sets and predicts \(1.7\), \(2.2\) and \(1.8\) at \(x_0\). Treating these three runs as the expectation over training sets, compute the expected squared error on a new noisy target at \(x_0\).

Show model answer

Mean prediction, squared bias and variance over the three training sets:

\[ \bar g = \frac{1.7 + 2.2 + 1.8}{3} = 1.9 \] \[ \text{Bias}^2 = (1.9 - 2.0)^2 = 0.01 \] \[ \text{Var} = \frac{0.04 + 0.09 + 0.01}{3} = 0.0467 \]

Expected squared error = bias² + variance + noise:

\[ 0.01 + 0.0467 + 0.25 = 0.307 \]

Две ловушки: забыть прибавить шум (получится 0.057) и поделить в variance на 2 вместо 3 (получится 0.33). Обе дают ответ вне допуска.

Урок 17 · Бритва Оккама · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Write the bias–variance decomposition of the expected squared error at a point \(x\). Define each term, state what the expectations are taken over, and say how the terms change with model complexity.

Show model answer

Let \(t = f(x) + \epsilon\), where the noise \(\epsilon\) has zero mean and variance \(\sigma^2\) and is independent of the training set. Let \(g_D\) be the model trained on training set \(D\), and \(\bar g(x) = \E_D[g_D(x)]\) its average prediction. Then

\[ \begin{gathered} \E\bigl[(t - g_D(x))^2\bigr] = \bigl(\bar g(x) - f(x)\bigr)^2 \\ {} + \E_D\bigl[(g_D(x) - \bar g(x))^2\bigr] + \sigma^2 . \end{gathered} \]

The expectations are taken over training sets \(D\) (and over the noise of the new target).

Bias²: systematic error of the average model; high for models that are too simple. Variance: sensitivity of the prediction to the particular training set; high for models that are too flexible. Noise \(\sigma^2\): irreducible, independent of the model.

Increasing complexity lowers the bias and raises the variance, so the expected error is U-shaped; the best complexity minimizes bias² + variance. More training data reduces the variance only.

Полный балл — за квадрат у bias, за третье слагаемое и за фразу «over training sets». Если на экзамене просят вывод, хватит двух шагов из степпера: отделить шум и добавить-вычесть \(\bar g\).

Урок 17 · Бритва Оккама · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A network clearly overfits: its training error is 0.01 and its validation error is 0.30. Which of the following are expected to reduce the variance term? Select all that apply.

  • Collecting more training data Variance убывает примерно как 1/N; bias при этом не меняется.
  • Reducing the number of hidden neurons Меньше capacity — меньше variance, ценой некоторого bias.
  • Adding weight decay Регуляризация ограничивает эффективную сложность.
  • Training for more epochs Дольше учить — точнее подгонять обучающие точки вместе с шумом: variance растёт.
  • Adding one more hidden layer Больше параметров — больше capacity: bias не выше, variance выше.
Show model answer

More data, fewer hidden neurons and weight decay. The other two options increase the effective complexity of the model and therefore its variance.

Вопрос проверяет, что ты связываешь overfitting именно с variance и помнишь четыре строки таблицы из раздела про trade-off.

Урок 17 · Бритва Оккама · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Four networks with \(J\) hidden neurons are trained on the same data. The noise variance of the targets is about 0.09. Which one do you select?

\(J\)Training MSEValidation MSE
20.280.30
50.100.11
200.040.15
1000.000.58
  • \(J = 2\) Обе ошибки высокие и почти равны: underfitting.
  • \(J = 5\) Минимальная validation MSE, и обе ошибки близки к уровню шума 0.09.
  • \(J = 20\) Training MSE уже вдвое ниже уровня шума, validation пошла вверх: начало overfitting.
  • \(J = 100\) Нулевая training MSE на шумных данных — признак того, что шум выучен.
Show model answer

\(J = 5\): it has the lowest validation error, and both of its errors are close to the noise level. \(J = 2\) underfits (both errors high), \(J = 20\) and \(J = 100\) overfit (training error well below the noise level, growing gap).

Выбирают по validation, а не по training error. Уровень шума в условии — подсказка: training MSE заметно ниже него означает подгонку под шум.

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели · вопрос 1 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Why is the training error not a good indicator of the performance of a model on future data? How can an independent test set be obtained?

Show model answer

Training error (loss) is not a good indicator of performance on future data for three reasons. (1) The model has been learned from the very same training data, so any estimate based on those data is optimistic. (2) New data will probably not be exactly the same as the training data. (3) Patterns can be found even in random data: a flexible model can fit pure noise and still reach a low training error.

Hence the model must be tested on an independent new test set. There are three ways to get one: someone provides a new dataset; the available data are split and some of them are hidden for later evaluation (hold-out); or the dataset is randomly subsampled with replacement, which is done when the dataset is small. In classification the class distribution must be preserved in every subset, i.e. stratified sampling.

Ответ идёт по слайду "How to Measure Generalization?" (лекция 3, слайд 4) почти слово в слово: три причины, вывод "We need to test on an independent new test set", три способа и строка про stratified sampling. Хороший пример к третьей причине — сети из загадки, которые подогнались под случайные метки.

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели · вопрос 2 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Define training dataset, training set, validation set and test set. Then explain why the validation error of the selected model is not a valid final estimate of its generalization error.

Show model answer

The training dataset is the available data: all the labelled samples we have. It is split into disjoint parts. The training set is the data used to learn the model parameters (weights and biases): parameter fitting. The validation set is the data used to perform model selection: choosing hyperparameters, comparing architectures and deciding when to stop training, without entering the gradient. The test set is the data used to perform the final model assessment: it is used only once, at the very end, to estimate the generalization error of the final model.

The validation error of the selected model is optimistically biased: the model was selected precisely because its validation error was the lowest among several noisy estimates, and the minimum of noisy estimates is biased downward. An unbiased estimate requires data that influenced no decision at all, i.e. the test set.

Проверяют три вещи: не перепутаны ли training dataset и training set (лекция 3, слайды 5 и 10); названа ли пара selection / assessment; и правильная причина оптимизма. Причина не в том, что «модель видела validation set» (в градиент он не входил), а в том, что по нему выбирали.

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели · вопрос 3 из 8 · открыть в уроке

According to the terminology of the course, which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.

  • The training dataset is all the available data, including the samples that will form the test set. На слайде: "Training dataset: the available data".
  • The training data are the data used to learn the model parameters. Это training set. Training data шире: "used to train the model (fitting + selection)".
  • The validation set belongs both to the training data and to the validation data. Selection входит в обе пары: fitting + selection и selection + assessment.
  • The validation data are used to assess the model quality: model selection and final assessment. Это validation set вместе с test set.
  • The test set is part of the training data. Test set входит в training dataset и в validation data, но не в training data.
Show model answer

The first, third and fourth. The training dataset is the available data. It is first split into training data (model development) and test data (pre-production). The training data are used to train the model (fitting + selection) and are split again into the training set, the data used to learn the model parameters, and the validation set, the data used to perform model selection. The validation data are the data used to assess the model quality (selection + assessment): the validation set together with the test set. The test set is used only for the final model assessment, so it never belongs to the training data.

Здесь проверяются шесть определений слайда "Clearing the terms …" (лекция 3, слайды 5 и 10): термины, которые отличаются одним словом. Вспомни схему с рис. 3 и читай ответы по ней.

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели · вопрос 4 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Is \(E_{\text{train}} \le E_{\text{test}}\) always true? Justify your answer.

Show model answer

No. It holds on average for a model obtained by minimising the training error. If \(\hat{\vb{w}}\) minimises \(E_{\text{train}}\) on the training set \(D\) and \(\vb{w}^{*}\) minimises the generalization error, then

\[ \begin{aligned} \E_D\bigl[E_{\text{train}}(\hat{\vb{w}})\bigr] &\le E_{\text{gen}}(\vb{w}^{*}) \\ &\le \E_D\bigl[E_{\text{gen}}(\hat{\vb{w}})\bigr] \end{aligned} \]

In a single run it can fail. The test set is finite, so its error is itself a noisy estimate and can be lower by chance, especially for a small test set. Moreover, with regularisers that act only at training time (dropout, data augmentation) the training error is often higher than the validation or test error.

Слово «always» в вопросе — приманка. Полный балл: «нет», затем «верно в среднем» с причиной (веса подобраны под эти примеры) и хотя бы один контрпример. Фраза слайда "any estimate based on that data will be optimistic" (лекция 3, слайд 4) этому не противоречит: она про смещение оценки, то есть про среднее, а не про каждый отдельный запуск.

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели · вопрос 5 из 8 · открыть в уроке

A classifier is evaluated on a hold-out validation set of 150 samples and reaches 90% accuracy. Compute the standard error of this estimate, in percentage points.

Show model answer
\[ \text{SE} = \sqrt{\frac{0.9 \cdot 0.1}{150}} = \sqrt{0.0006} \approx 0.0245 \]

That is about 2.45 percentage points: a 95% interval is roughly \(90\% \pm 4.9\%\). Two models whose accuracies differ by 2 points cannot be reliably told apart with this validation set.

Типичные потери: подставить в формулу 90 и 10 вместо долей 0.9 и 0.1 или забыть корень. Вывод про «две модели не различить» стоит дописать и в открытом вопросе: он показывает, что число понято, а не только посчитано.

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели · вопрос 6 из 8 · открыть в уроке

A binary dataset has 50 samples, 5 of which are positive. A test set of 10 samples is drawn at random, without stratification. What is the probability that the test set contains no positive sample?

Show model answer

Each of the 5 positives must fall into one of the 40 training positions:

\[ \frac{40}{50}\cdot\frac{39}{49}\cdot\frac{38}{48}\cdot\frac{37}{47}\cdot\frac{36}{46} \approx 0.31 \]

In almost one split out of three the test set says nothing about the positive class. Stratified sampling keeps the class proportions in every subset: here exactly 1 positive out of 10.

Пять дробей считаются на обычном калькуляторе. Экзаменатор ждёт не только число, но и вывод: зачем нужен stratified sampling.

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели · вопрос 7 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following practices leak information and make the reported performance optimistic? Select all that apply.

  • Standardising the features with the mean and standard deviation of the whole dataset, and splitting afterwards. В \(\mu\) и \(\sigma\) вошли примеры из validation и test set.
  • Choosing the number of training epochs as the one with the lowest test error. Гиперпараметр выбран по test set: тот стал вторым validation set.
  • Stopping the training when the validation error stops decreasing. Это штатная работа validation set — model selection.
  • Augmenting the images first and splitting the augmented dataset afterwards. Копии одной картинки попадают и в training, и в test set.
  • After the hyperparameters are chosen, retraining on the training set + validation set and evaluating once on the test set. Решения уже приняты, test set ни на что не влиял.
Show model answer

The first, second and fourth. In each of them data that are later used for evaluation have influenced the model: through the preprocessing statistics, through a hyperparameter, or directly through the weights (duplicates). Early stopping on the validation set and retraining on the whole training data (training set + validation set) after selection are legitimate, provided the final number is measured once on an untouched test set.

Правило для любого варианта: спроси, откуда пришла информация для решения. Если из данных, на которых потом ставят оценку, это утечка.

Урок 18 · Честный экзамен для модели · вопрос 8 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Describe hold-out validation. What are the two sources of error of the hold-out estimate, and when is hold-out an appropriate choice?

Show model answer

The available data are shuffled and split once into disjoint training, validation and test sets (e.g. 70/15/15). The model is trained on the training set, tuned on the validation set (hyperparameters, early stopping) and assessed once on the test set. In classification the split is stratified, i.e. it preserves the class distribution.

The estimate has (i) a pessimistic bias, because the model is trained on only a fraction of the data, and (ii) a variance, because it is computed on a finite held-out set and depends on which samples fell into it: in the words of the slides, the hold-out error "might be biased by the specific split". For an error rate \(p\) measured on \(n\) samples the standard error is about \(\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}\). Holding out more samples reduces the variance but increases the bias, and vice versa.

Hold-out is appropriate when lots of data are available (thousands of samples or more) and training is expensive, so that one training per configuration is all one can afford: the usual situation in deep learning. With few data, cross-validation (leave-one-out or K-fold) is preferable.

На слайде зависимость от разреза названа словом "biased" (лекция 3, слайд 6), поэтому в ответе стоят обе вещи: фраза слайда и точные термины — variance для зависимости от разреза, bias для систематического пессимизма. Последняя фраза про cross-validation показывает, что ты видишь границу метода.

Урок 19 · Экзамен по кругу

Урок 19 · Экзамен по кругу · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Compare hold-out validation, K-fold cross-validation and leave-one-out cross-validation. For each, state the number of trainings per configuration, the bias and the variance of the error estimate, and when you would use it.

Show model answer

Hold-out: 1 training. The model is trained on a fraction of the data (e.g. 70–80%), so the estimate is pessimistically biased. The hold-out error also "might be biased by the specific split": another split gives another number, so the variance of the estimate is high when the validation set is small. Use it when lots of data are available or training is expensive (the usual case in deep learning).

K-fold (K = 5 or 10): K trainings. Each model sees \((K-1)/K\) of the data: small pessimistic bias. All \(N\) samples are used for validation once and K errors are averaged: moderate variance. A good trade-off between the other two, sometimes even better than LOOCV: far fewer trainings and a less noisy estimate.

LOOCV (K = N): N trainings. Each model is trained on \(N-1\) of the \(N\) samples, so the estimate is unbiased (strictly, almost unbiased: the final model is trained on all \(N\)). But it has high variance (each term rests on a single sample and the terms are highly correlated) and it is unfeasible with lots of data. Use it only when few data are available and the model is cheap to train.

In all cases the selected configuration is finally retrained on all the available data.

Проверяют три оси сразу: цена, bias, variance. Обязательно назови направление bias (пессимистичный, потому что меньше данных) и причину, по которой LOOCV почти несмещённая: \(N - 1\) примеров в обучении, а не «каждый пример проверяет один раз».

Каркас ответа — три строки слайдов 6–8 лекции 3: много данных — hold-out, мало — LOOCV, K-fold — компромисс. Фразы слайдов, которые стоят в ответе, узнают сразу; уточнения к ним показывают, что ты понимаешь, откуда они.

Урок 19 · Экзамен по кругу · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A model with no inputs predicts a constant, \(g(x \mid w) = w_0\). It is fitted by minimising the sum of squared errors, so \(w_0\) is the mean of the training targets. The dataset has three samples with targets \(t = 2,\ 4,\ 9\). Compute the leave-one-out cross-validation estimate of the mean squared error.

Show model answer

Leave out 2: \(w_0 = 6.5\), error \((2 - 6.5)^2 = 20.25\). Leave out 4: \(w_0 = 5.5\), error \(2.25\). Leave out 9: \(w_0 = 3\), error \(36\).

\[ \hat E_{\text{LOOCV}} = \frac{20.25 + 2.25 + 36}{3} = 19.5 \]

Check: the training MSE on all three samples is \(26/3 = 8.67\), and \(8.67 \cdot (3/2)^2 = 19.5\).

Типичная ошибка — посчитать среднее по всем трём (5) и получить training MSE 8.67. В каждом раунде среднее берётся только по двум оставшимся примерам.

Урок 19 · Экзамен по кругу · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

You run K-fold cross-validation on a training dataset of \(N\) samples. (a) Write the error \(\hat e_k\) of fold \(k\) and the cross-validation estimate \(\hat E\), and define every symbol. (b) At the end you have \(K\) trained models. What do you do with all these models?

Show model answer

(a) The training dataset is split into \(K\) disjoint folds; \(N_k\) is the set of samples of fold \(k\). In round \(k\) the model is trained on the other \(K-1\) folds and validated on fold \(k\):

\[ \hat e_k = \frac{1}{|N_k|}\sum_{n \in N_k} E\big(x_n \mid w^{(k)}\big) \] \[ \hat E = \frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K} \hat e_k \]

\(E(x_n \mid w)\) is the error on sample \(n\), e.g. \((t_n - g(x_n \mid w))^2\); \(w^{(k)}\) (simply \(w\) on the slide) are the weights learned in round \(k\), without fold \(k\); \(\hat E\) estimates the error on new data.

(b) None of them is the final model: they are by-products of the estimate. Each has seen only \((K-1)/K\) of the data, and a low error on one fold mostly means an easy fold. Cross-validation assesses the recipe (architecture, hyperparameters, training procedure), not a single network. I keep \(\hat E\), use it to select the hyperparameters, then put all the data back together and train one final model on the whole training dataset. An alternative is to keep all \(K\) models and average their outputs as an ensemble; \(\hat E\) then describes a single member, not the ensemble.

Обе формулы и вопрос (b) — со слайда 9 лекции 3. В ответе нужны три вещи: деление на \(|N_k|\) (среднее по fold, а не сумма), пояснение, что веса в каждом раунде свои, и обучение заново на всех данных (на слайде 16 лекции 3: "Put back all data together and minimize"). Последнее предложение про ensemble — сверх слайдов, его можно опустить.

Урок 19 · Экзамен по кругу · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

You run nested cross-validation with \(K = 10\) outer folds, \(M = 5\) inner folds and 8 candidate configurations. How many networks are trained to obtain the nested estimate, including the retraining of the selected configuration on each outer training set?

Show model answer
\[ K\,(M\,|\Theta| + 1) = 10 \cdot (5 \cdot 8 + 1) = 410 \]

Producing the final deployable model costs \(M|\Theta| + 1 = 41\) more trainings: one selection on all the data and one final training.

Формула конспекта \(K \times M \times |\Theta|\) дала бы 400: она теряет по одному переобучению на каждый outer fold.

Урок 19 · Экзамен по кругу · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Which statements about K-fold cross-validation are correct? Select all that apply.

  • Each sample is used for validation exactly once. Folds не пересекаются и вместе покрывают все данные.
  • Increasing K increases the fraction of data used for training in each round. Доля равна \((K-1)/K\): 50% при \(K = 2\), 90% при \(K = 10\).
  • The final model is the one of the K models with the lowest fold error. K моделей — побочный продукт. Итоговую модель обучают заново на всех данных.
  • With K = N it coincides with leave-one-out cross-validation. Каждый fold состоит из одного примера.
  • Feature standardisation should be fitted once on the whole dataset before the folds are made. Это утечка: validation fold влияет на preprocessing. Параметры считают по \(K - 1\) учебным folds в каждом раунде.
Show model answer

Correct: the first, the second and the fourth statements. The K models are by-products of the estimate, and any preprocessing must be re-fitted inside each fold on the training folds only.

Две неверные опции — самые частые ошибки на практике: «взять лучшую из K сетей» и «нормализовать до разбиения».

Урок 19 · Экзамен по кругу · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

You evaluate 10 architectures with 5-fold cross-validation, select the one with the lowest average validation error (0.15) and report 0.15 as the expected error on new data. (a) Why is this estimate optimistic, although no weight was ever trained on the fold it was validated on? (b) How does nested cross-validation fix it? (c) Which model do you finally deploy?

Show model answer

(a) The validation folds were used for model selection. Each CV score is a noisy estimate of the true error, and we report the minimum of 10 noisy estimates: \(\E[\min_\theta \hat E_\theta] \le \min_\theta E_\theta\). The choice is tuned to those particular folds, so the score no longer represents unseen data.

(b) Nested CV separates selection from assessment. The outer loop holds out a test fold; the inner loop runs M-fold CV on the remaining data only and selects the configuration; the selected configuration is retrained on the whole outer training set and evaluated on the outer test fold, which played no part in the choice. The average of the K outer test errors estimates the error of the whole selection procedure.

(c) Nested CV returns an estimate, not a model. For deployment, run the selection once on all the data, retrain the selected configuration on all the data, and report the nested estimate as its expected error.

Ключевые слова: model selection против model assessment, «minimum of noisy estimates», «inner loop selects, outer loop assesses». Пункт (c) чаще всего забывают. Слов nested cross-validation на слайдах нет, поэтому вопрос могут задать и без них, в словах слайда: K-fold применяют и для model assessment, и, чаще, для model selection (слайд 10 лекции 3).

Урок 19 · Экзамен по кругу · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

You standardise all the features using the mean and the standard deviation computed on the whole dataset, and then run 5-fold cross-validation. What is wrong, and how do you fix it?

Show model answer

This is data leakage: the statistics used for preprocessing were computed also on the samples that later act as validation folds, so the validation data influenced the training pipeline and the estimate becomes optimistic. Fix: inside each round, compute the mean and the standard deviation on the \(K-1\) training folds only and apply them to the held-out fold (and, at the very end, to the test set).

Достаточно двух предложений: назвать утечку и сказать «fit inside each fold on the training folds only».

Урок 20 · Когда нажать «стоп»

Урок 20 · Когда нажать «стоп» · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Describe early stopping as seen in class. List the steps of the procedure, describe the plot of the training set error and of the validation set error against the number of gradient descent iterations \(k\), say what \(k_{ES}\) and \(E_{ES}(x \mid w)\) are on that plot, and explain what the validation set error is an online estimate of.

Show model answer

Early stopping limits overfitting by cross-validation. Four steps: hold out some data; train on the training set; perform cross-validation on the hold-out set, i.e. compute the error on it while training; stop training when the validation error increases.

The plot has the number of gradient descent iterations \(k\) on the horizontal axis and the error \(E(x \mid w)\) on the vertical axis. The training set error has a monotone decreasing trend (on average with SGD). The validation set error decreases with it at first, reaches a minimum and then increases: from that point on the network loses generalization, i.e. it overfits. \(k_{ES}\) is the iteration at which the validation set error is minimal, and \(E_{ES}(x \mid w)\) is the validation set error at that iteration. The weights to keep are those of \(k_{ES}\).

The hold-out data are not used to compute the gradient, so the validation set error estimates the generalization error. The estimate is online: it is obtained while training, without any additional training run.

Это слайд 12 лекции 3, пересказанный словами: четыре шага, две кривые, \(k_{ES}\), \(E_{ES}\) и online estimate. Слово cross-validation здесь значит hold-out, а не K-fold. Как четвёртый шаг выполняют на шумной кривой — patience и возврат к лучшим весам — спрашивает следующий вопрос.

Урок 20 · Когда нажать «стоп» · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Define early stopping. What is \(k_{ES}\), what is the patience, which weights are returned at the end, and why is early stopping considered a regulariser although the error function is not modified?

Show model answer

Some data are held out and the network is trained on the training set only. While training, the error on the hold-out (validation) set, \(E_{\mathrm{val}}(k)\), is computed after every epoch \(k\) together with the training error. The training error keeps decreasing (on average with SGD); the validation error decreases and then increases, when the network starts fitting the noise of the training set and loses generalization. Training is stopped when the validation error increases, and early stopping returns the weights of the epoch

\[ k_{ES} = \argmin_{k}\; E_{\mathrm{val}}(k) , \]

where the validation error takes its lowest value, \(E_{ES} = E_{\mathrm{val}}(k_{ES})\).

Because the validation curve is noisy, training is not stopped at the first increase: it is stopped when \(E_{\mathrm{val}}\) has not improved on its best value for \(P\) consecutive epochs (the patience). A checkpoint of the weights is saved at every new best value and restored at the end, so the model returned is the best one, not the last one.

It is a regulariser because limiting the number of gradient descent steps limits how far the weights can move from their small initial values: the effective capacity is reduced although no weight is removed. For a quadratic error it is approximately equivalent to weight decay with a coefficient \(\gamma\) proportional to \(1/(\eta\tau)\): the fewer the steps, the stronger the regularisation. It requires a validation set, never the test set.

Вопрос проверяет четыре вещи: кривые и \(\argmin\), слова «best value so far» в определении patience, возврат к checkpoint и ответ на «why» через effective capacity. Одной строки слайда 12 лекции 3 "Stop train when validation error increases" здесь мало: вопрос прямо спрашивает про patience и возвращаемые веса.

Урок 20 · Когда нажать «стоп» · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

The validation error at epochs 10 to 17 is 0.40, 0.38, 0.37, 0.375, 0.373, 0.376, 0.379, 0.382; epoch 10 improves on all earlier epochs. Early stopping is used with patience 2: training stops as soon as the validation error has not improved on its best value for 2 consecutive epochs. After which epoch does training stop?

Show model answer

The best value is 0.37 at epoch 12. Epoch 13 (0.375) does not improve on it: wait = 1. Epoch 14 (0.373) is lower than epoch 13 but still higher than the best value: wait = 2. Training stops after epoch 14 and the weights of epoch 12 are restored.

With patience 4 it would stop after epoch 16, restoring the same weights.

Ловушка — эпоха 14: она лучше предыдущей, но не лучше рекорда. Если в условии patience определён иначе, считай по условию и одной фразой запиши, какое правило используешь. Если patience в условии нет совсем, а правило дано строкой слайда 12 лекции 3, это остановка на первом росте: здесь — после эпохи 13, с весами эпохи 12.

Урок 20 · Когда нажать «стоп» · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

The validation error at epochs 1 to 10 is 0.90, 0.70, 0.55, 0.57, 0.50, 0.52, 0.51, 0.53, 0.54, 0.56. Early stopping with patience 3 and checkpoint restore is used. The weights of which epoch are returned?

Show model answer

Epochs 1, 2, 3 are improvements (best 0.55). Epoch 4: wait = 1. Epoch 5 (0.50) is a new best: checkpoint saved, wait = 0. Epochs 6, 7, 8 (0.52, 0.51, 0.53) do not improve on 0.50: wait = 3, so training stops after epoch 8. The weights of epoch 5 are returned.

With patience 1, i.e. stopping at the first increase of the validation error, training would stop after epoch 4 and return epoch 3, with \(E_{ES} = 0.55\) instead of 0.50.

Два неверных ответа выдают две разные ошибки: 3 — остановка на первом росте, 8 — «возвращается последняя эпоха».

Урок 20 · Когда нажать «стоп» · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Which statements about early stopping are correct? Select all that apply.

  • It requires a validation set that is not used to compute the gradient. Без отложенных данных кривой \(E_{\mathrm{val}}(k)\) просто нет.
  • The model returned is the one obtained at the last epoch trained. Возвращается checkpoint с наименьшей validation error; последняя эпоха на \(P\) эпох позже.
  • It reduces the number of weights of the network. Веса все на месте; уменьшается effective capacity.
  • For the same training run, a larger patience with checkpoint restore never returns a model with a higher validation error. Рекорд на более длинном отрезке не может быть хуже рекорда на его начале. Дороже только вычисления.
  • The validation error \(E_{ES}\) at \(k_{ES}\) is an unbiased estimate of the generalisation error. Оценка — да, так и на слайде 12 лекции 3. Несмещённая — нет: она занижена, эпоху выбирали по этим же данным.
  • In the terminology of the course it limits overfitting by cross-validation: the error on a hold-out set is monitored while training. Так называется слайд 12 лекции 3: cross-validation включает и hold-out (слайд 6 лекции 3); K-fold здесь ни при чём.
Show model answer

Correct: the first, the fourth and the sixth statements. Early stopping needs held-out data; the best checkpoint, not the last epoch, is returned; no weight is removed; the selected validation error is optimistically biased; monitoring the error on a hold-out set is cross-validation in the sense used in the course.

Четвёртое утверждение говорит про validation error, а не про ошибку на новых данных: вторая при большем patience может и вырасти, как в прогоне № 2 симулятора.

Урок 20 · Когда нажать «стоп» · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A network is trained with early stopping and the value \(E_{ES} = E_{\mathrm{val}}(k_{ES})\), the validation error at the stopping point, is reported as its generalisation error. Explain why this number is optimistically biased and how an unbiased estimate is obtained.

Show model answer

For an epoch fixed in advance the validation error is an unbiased estimate of the generalisation error, because the hold-out data are not used to compute the gradient: this is the online estimate given by the validation curve. But \(k_{ES}\) is chosen as the minimum of that curve, so the validation set has been used to make a choice: it is part of model selection. Each \(E_{\mathrm{val}}(k)\) is the true error plus noise due to the finite validation set; the minimum over many epochs tends to be attained where the noise is negative, hence on average

\[ \E\Bigl[\min_k E_{\mathrm{val}}(k)\Bigr] \le \min_k \E\bigl[E_{\mathrm{val}}(k)\bigr] . \]

An unbiased estimate requires data used neither for training nor for any selection: a separate test set, the data reserved for the final model assessment, evaluated once on the final model.

Ключевые слова: «used for selection», «minimum of noisy estimates», «separate test set». Ответ «потому что сеть обучалась на validation set» неверен: градиент по нему не считали.

Урок 20 · Когда нажать «стоп» · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Early stopping, weight decay and dropout all reduce the effective capacity of a neural network. Say what each of them constrains, name one hyperparameter of each, and explain the difference between nominal and effective capacity.

Show model answer

Early stopping (training control; in the slides, limiting overfitting by cross-validation) limits the number of training iterations, i.e. how far the weights can move from their initial values; hyperparameter: the patience. Weight decay (model complexity control; by weights regularization) adds a penalty on the squared weights to the error function and limits how large the weights grow; hyperparameter: the coefficient \(\gamma\). Dropout (randomisation, model averaging; by stochastic regularization) switches off random neurons at every training step, so that units cannot rely on specific other units; hyperparameter: the dropout probability.

The nominal capacity is the set of functions the architecture can represent with some weights; it is not changed, since no weight is removed. The effective capacity is the set of functions that training can actually reach; this is what the three techniques reduce. Their hyperparameters are chosen on validation data.

Обзорный вопрос ко всему блоку про overfitting (лекция 3, слайды 11–22). Главная ловушка — фраза «regularisation reduces the number of parameters»: число весов не меняется. В скобках у каждого метода два имени: родовое и подзаголовок слайдов 12, 14 и 17 лекции 3.

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама · вопрос 1 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Explain the difference between the parameters and the hyperparameters of a Feed Forward Neural Network. Why can the number of hidden neurons or the regularization strength \(\gamma\) not be selected by minimising the training error? What is the test set needed for?

Show model answer

Model selection happens at two levels. At the parameters level we learn the weights \(\vb{w}\) (biases included): gradient descent minimises the error function \(E(\vb{w})\) on the training set. At the hyperparameters level we choose the number of layers \(L\), the number of hidden neurons \(J^{(l)}\) of each layer \(l\) and the other settings that are fixed before training (learning rate, batch size, \(\gamma\), dropout rate); they control how learning proceeds and how complex the model can become.

The training error decreases as model capacity grows, so minimising it would always select the largest network and \(\gamma = 0\), i.e. the model that overfits most. Hyperparameters are therefore selected on data not used to fit the weights: we choose the model with the best validation error,

\[ \theta^{*} = \argmin_{\theta}\, E_{\mathrm{val}}(\theta) . \]

The validation error of the selected configuration is the minimum of several noisy estimates, hence optimistically biased. An independent test set, used once at the end for the final model assessment, gives an unbiased estimate of the generalization error.

В полном ответе три вещи: кто подбирает каждую группу чисел и на каких данных; training error только убывает с ростом сложности; оценка после отбора смещена. Ответ «потому что так делают cross-validation» ничего не объясняет: нужна сама причина — по training error всегда победит самая сложная модель. Рамка первого абзаца — со слайда (лекция 3, слайд 13).

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама · вопрос 2 из 8 · открыть в уроке

You have to select the number of hidden neurons \(J^{(1)}\) of a network with one hidden layer. Describe the procedure, which relies on a validation set and on early stopping. Which quantity is compared across the candidate models? How does the procedure change when the hyperparameter is the weight decay coefficient \(\gamma\), and what do you do once the best value has been found?

Show model answer

Split the data in a training set and a validation set. For each candidate value of \(J^{(1)}\) train the network on the training set with early stopping: the training error keeps decreasing with the iterations \(k\), while the validation error reaches its minimum at \(k_{ES}\). Record the validation error at that point, \(E_{ES}^{J^{(1)}}(x|w)\): it estimates the generalization error with \(J^{(1)}\) neurons in one layer. Choose the model with the best (lowest) validation error.

For \(\gamma\) the scheme is the same. For different values of \(\gamma\) minimise on the training set

\[ \begin{aligned} E_{\gamma}^{TRAIN} = {} & \sum_{n=1}^{N_{TRAIN}} \bigl(t_n - g(x_n|\vb{w})\bigr)^2 \\ & + \gamma \sum_{q=1}^{Q} (w_q)^2 , \end{aligned} \]

then evaluate each model on the validation set, without the penalty,

\[ E_{\gamma}^{VAL} = \sum_{n=1}^{N_{VAL}} \bigl(t_n - g(x_n|\vb{w})\bigr)^2 , \]

and choose the \(\gamma^{*}\) with the best validation error. Finally put all the data back together and minimise again with \(\gamma^{*}\).

The validation error of the winner has been used to make the choice, so the final assessment of the selected model requires a separate test set.

Опорные фразы слайдов: "Chose model with best validation error" (лекция 3, слайд 13) и "Put back all data together and minimize" (лекция 3, слайд 16); пиши "choose", а не "chose". Две детали теряют чаще всего: в \(E_{\gamma}^{VAL}\) нет штрафа, а после выбора сеть обучают заново на всех данных.

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама · вопрос 3 из 8 · открыть в уроке

A network with one hidden layer is trained with early stopping for four values of \(J^{(1)}\). The validation errors at the early-stopping point are

\[ E_{ES}^{1} = 0.55, \quad E_{ES}^{2} = 0.38, \] \[ E_{ES}^{5} = 0.21, \quad E_{ES}^{9} = 0.24, \]

and the training errors at the same points are 0.52, 0.33, 0.14 and 0.06. Which model do you select?

  • \(J^{(1)} = 1\): its training and validation errors are the closest. Маленький разрыв говорит только о том, что нет overfitting. Обе ошибки здесь велики: это underfitting. Выбирают по самой validation error, а не по разрыву.
  • \(J^{(1)} = 5\): it has the best validation error. \(E_{ES}^{5} = 0.21\) — наименьшая из четырёх. Это правило со слайда (лекция 3, слайд 13): "Chose model with best validation error".
  • \(J^{(1)} = 9\): it has the lowest training error. Training error убывает с ростом сети всегда, по ней победит самая большая модель. А её validation error уже выше: 0.24 против 0.21.
  • None of them yet: the choice must be made on the test set. Test set в выборе не участвует. Он нужен один раз, для итоговой оценки уже выбранной модели.
Show model answer

\(J^{(1)} = 5\): choose the model with the best validation error, \(E_{ES}^{5} = 0.21\). The training error cannot be used for the choice: it keeps decreasing as the network grows.

Числа повторяют график слайда (лекция 3, слайд 13).

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама · вопрос 4 из 8 · открыть в уроке

A grid search explores 4 hyperparameters with 5 candidate values each. Every configuration is evaluated with 5-fold cross-validation. How many networks are trained in total?

Show model answer
\[ |\Theta| = 5^4 = 625 \ \mathrm{configurations} \] \[ 625 \cdot 5 = 3125 \ \mathrm{trainings} \]

Две типичные ошибки: сложить вместо того, чтобы перемножить (\(4 \cdot 5 = 20\)), и забыть множитель \(K\) от cross-validation.

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама · вопрос 5 из 8 · открыть в уроке

A random search draws \(N = 20\) independent configurations. What is the probability that at least one of them falls in the best 5% of the search space? Give a number between 0 and 1.

Show model answer

Each trial misses the region with probability \(0.95\); the trials are independent.

\[ P = 1 - 0.95^{20} = 1 - 0.358 = 0.642 \]

Считается через противоположное событие «все двадцать мимо». На калькуляторе: клавиша \(x^y\). Ответ \(20 \cdot 0.05 = 1\) — признак того, что вероятности сложены, а так можно только для несовместных событий.

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама · вопрос 6 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Which statements about hyperparameter search are correct? Select all that apply.

  • The number of trainings required by grid search grows exponentially with the number of hyperparameters. Размер сетки — произведение \(\prod_j n_j\).
  • Random search uses the results of previous trials to choose the next configuration. Это свойство Bayesian optimisation. Random и grid слепые: каждая проба не зависит от остальных.
  • With the same budget, random search tries more distinct values of each single hyperparameter than grid search. \(N\) разных значений против \(n\) у сетки \(n \times n\).
  • Bayesian optimisation fits a surrogate model of the validation error and picks the next trial by maximising an acquisition function. Это определение метода: surrogate плюс acquisition.
  • The validation error of the selected configuration is an unbiased estimate of the generalization error. Она занижена: это минимум из многих шумных оценок. Нужен test set.
Show model answer

Correct: the first, the third and the fourth statement.

Каждое утверждение проверяй отдельно, как вопрос «верно или нет», и отмечай только то, что можешь обосновать одной фразой. Две ловушки здесь построены на перестановке: свойство Bayesian optimisation приписано random search, а свойство test error — validation error.

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама · вопрос 7 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Compare grid search, random search and Bayesian optimisation for hyperparameter tuning. Why is random search usually preferred to grid search when the budget of trainings is the same?

Show model answer

All three minimise the validation error \(E_{\mathrm{val}}(\theta)\) with the same loop (propose a configuration, train, evaluate); they differ in how the next configuration is proposed.

Grid search evaluates every combination of predefined values: simple and parallelisable, but the cost \(\prod_j n_j\) is exponential in the number of hyperparameters. Random search samples \(N\) configurations from chosen distributions (log-uniform for \(\eta\) and \(\gamma\)): the budget \(N\) is chosen freely and the trials run in parallel, but it ignores previous trials and may miss the good region by chance. Bayesian optimisation fits a probabilistic surrogate model to the past trials and chooses the next configuration by maximising an acquisition function that balances exploration (uncertain regions) and exploitation (promising regions): far fewer trainings, but more machinery and inherently sequential.

Random search is preferred to grid search because the validation error usually depends strongly on only a few hyperparameters. An \(n \times n\) grid tests only \(n\) distinct values of the important one, while \(N = n^2\) random trials test \(N\) distinct values of every hyperparameter.

Ключевая фраза для полного балла — про число разных значений важного hyperparameter. Простое «random is more efficient» ничего не объясняет.

Урок 21 · Ручки, которые сеть не крутит сама · вопрос 8 из 8 · открыть в уроке

The mini-batch size \(M\) is reduced from 64 to 4, everything else unchanged. The mini-batch estimate of the gradient becomes…

  • more accurate, because fewer samples are averaged. Наоборот: чем меньше слагаемых в среднем, тем больше его разброс.
  • noisier (higher variance), but still unbiased. Дисперсия выборочного среднего растёт как \(1/M\), а его математическое ожидание не меняется.
  • biased towards zero. Смещения нет при любом \(M\): в среднем оценка равна настоящему градиенту.
  • identical: the batch size only changes the speed. Скорость одного шага меняется, но меняется и шум оценки.
Show model answer

Noisier but still unbiased: the estimate is a sample mean of \(M\) per-sample gradients, its variance scales as \(1/M\).

Слова со слайда, которые стоит использовать в открытом ответе (лекция 2, слайд 28): unbiased, high variance, trade off variance-computation.

Урок 22 · Пружины на весах

Урок 22 · Пружины на весах · вопрос 1 из 6 · открыть в уроке

What is regularization? Write the maximum likelihood and the maximum a-posteriori estimates of the weights of a neural network and explain how the second one limits overfitting. Which a-priori distribution of the weights leads to weight decay, and why is it chosen?

Show model answer

Regularization constrains the freedom of the model, on the basis of an a-priori assumption on the model, in order to reduce overfitting.

Maximum likelihood chooses the weights that make the observed data most probable; nothing is assumed about the weights themselves:

\[ \vb{w}_{\mathrm{MLE}} = \argmax_{\vb{w}}\; P(D|\vb{w}) \]

The Bayesian approach reduces the freedom of the model by making an assumption on the a-priori distribution of the parameters, \(P(\vb{w})\), and by maximising the posterior instead of the likelihood (Bayes' rule; \(P(D)\) does not depend on \(\vb{w}\)):

\[ \begin{aligned} \vb{w}_{\mathrm{MAP}} &= \argmax_{\vb{w}}\; P(\vb{w}|D) \\ &= \argmax_{\vb{w}}\; P(D|\vb{w})\,P(\vb{w}) \end{aligned} \]

Weights that are improbable under the prior are selected only if the data support them strongly, so the network is no longer free to fit the noise. Weight decay assumes that the weights are small: every weight is independently distributed as \(w_q \sim N(0, \sigma_w^2)\). The motivation is empirical: neural networks with small weights are observed to generalize better.

Ответ идёт в порядке слайда 14 лекции 3: определение, MLE, MAP, prior. Две типичные недоработки: определение без слов про априорное предположение («регуляризация борется с переобучением» называет цель, а не способ) и prior без нулевого среднего — именно ноль в \(N(0, \sigma_w^2)\) означает «веса малы».

Урок 22 · Пружины на весах · вопрос 2 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Weight decay. (a) Write the error function that is minimised when a regression network is trained with weight decay. (b) Derive it as a Maximum A-Posteriori (MAP) estimate, stating the assumptions. (c) How is \(\gamma\) related to the variances involved? What happens when \(\sigma_w \to \infty\), and when the number of samples \(N\) grows?

Show model answer

(a) With \(\gamma \gt 0\) as the regularisation parameter and \(q = 1, \dots, Q\) indexing all the weights of the network:

\[ \begin{aligned} E(\vb{w}) = {} & \sum_{n=1}^{N} \big(t_n - g(x_n|\vb{w})\big)^2 \\ & + \gamma \sum_{q=1}^{Q} w_q^2 \end{aligned} \]

The first term is the fitting term, the second one the regularization term.

(b) Assumptions: \(t_n = g(x_n|\vb{w}) + \epsilon_n\) with independent \(\epsilon_n \sim N(0, \sigma^2)\); independent zero-mean Gaussian prior on every weight, \(w_q \sim N(0, \sigma_w^2)\). Since \(P(D)\) does not depend on \(\vb{w}\):

\[ \begin{aligned} \vb{w}_{\mathrm{MAP}} &= \argmax_{\vb{w}}\; P(\vb{w}|D) \\ &= \argmax_{\vb{w}}\; P(D|\vb{w})\,P(\vb{w}) \end{aligned} \]

Both factors are products of Gaussians, one per sample and one per weight. Writing \(g_n\) for \(g(x_n|\vb{w})\):

\[ \begin{aligned} \vb{w}_{\mathrm{MAP}} = \argmax_{\vb{w}}\; &\prod_{n=1}^{N} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,\sigma}\; e^{-\frac{(t_n - g_n)^2}{2\sigma^2}} \\ {}\cdot{} &\prod_{q=1}^{Q} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,\sigma_w}\; e^{-\frac{w_q^2}{2\sigma_w^2}} \end{aligned} \]

Taking the negative logarithm (it is monotone: products become sums, the maximum becomes a minimum) and dropping the terms that do not depend on \(\vb{w}\):

\[ \begin{aligned} \vb{w}_{\mathrm{MAP}} = \argmin_{\vb{w}} \Big[ &\sum_{n=1}^{N} \frac{(t_n - g_n)^2}{2\sigma^2} \\ &+ \sum_{q=1}^{Q} \frac{w_q^2}{2\sigma_w^2} \Big] \end{aligned} \]

Multiplying by \(2\sigma^2 \gt 0\) gives the error function of (a).

(c) \(\gamma = \sigma^2 / \sigma_w^2\): noise variance over prior variance. Noisier data or a narrower prior give a stronger regularisation. For \(\sigma_w \to \infty\) the prior is flat, \(\gamma \to 0\), and the MAP estimate coincides with the maximum likelihood estimate. \(\gamma\) does not depend on \(N\), but the fitting term is a sum of \(N\) squares while the penalty stays fixed: as \(N\) grows the data dominate the prior and the MAP estimate again approaches the maximum likelihood one.

Вывод в ответе повторяет четыре строки слайда 15 лекции 3; \(\gamma = \sigma^2 / \sigma_w^2\) там не написано — его выводят из двух последних строк. Проверяют три вещи: что названы оба предположения (независимый гауссов шум и независимый Gaussian prior с нулевым средним), что объяснено, почему можно отбросить \(P(D)\) и взять логарифм, и что \(\gamma\) названа отношением дисперсий. Ответ «\(\gamma = 1/\sigma^2\)» без пояснения, чья это дисперсия, читается как «дисперсия шума в знаменателе», а это неверно.

Урок 22 · Пружины на весах · вопрос 3 из 6 · открыть в уроке

A network is trained by gradient descent on the regularised error

\[ E_{\mathrm{reg}}(\vb{w}) = E_{\mathrm{train}}(\vb{w}) + \frac{\gamma}{2} \sum_q w_q^2 . \]

Write the update rule for one weight and compute its new value after one step, given \(w = 2\), \(\partial E_{\mathrm{train}} / \partial w = 0.5\), \(\eta = 0.1\), \(\gamma = 0.01\).

Show model answer
\[ w \leftarrow (1 - \eta\gamma)\, w - \eta\, \pd{E_{\mathrm{train}}}{w} \] \[ w = 0.999 \cdot 2 - 0.1 \cdot 0.5 = 1.948 \]

Each step first shrinks the weight by the factor \(1 - \eta\gamma\), then applies the usual gradient step: hence the name "weight decay".

Здесь штраф записан с \(\frac{\gamma}{2}\), как в конспекте, поэтому множитель равен \(1 - \eta\gamma\). С записью \(\gamma \sum_q w_q^2\), как на слайде 15 лекции 3, получилось бы \(1 - 2\eta\gamma\) и ответ 1.946. Ловушка — применить заученную формулу, не посмотрев на условие.

Урок 22 · Пружины на весах · вопрос 4 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Describe the procedure used to select \(\gamma\). Is the penalty term included when the validation error is computed? Why can \(\gamma\) not be selected by minimising the training error?

Show model answer

\(\gamma\) is a hyperparameter and is selected by cross-validation on held-out data. Split the data into a training set and a validation set (the test set is kept aside). For different values of \(\gamma\), usually taken on a logarithmic grid such as \(10^{-5}, \dots, 10\), train on the training set by minimising the error with the penalty:

\[ \begin{aligned} E_{\gamma}^{\mathrm{TRAIN}} = {} & \sum_{n=1}^{N_{\mathrm{TRAIN}}} \big(t_n - g(x_n|\vb{w})\big)^2 \\ & + \gamma \sum_{q=1}^{Q} w_q^2 \end{aligned} \]

Evaluate each trained model on the validation set with the plain sum of squared errors, without the penalty:

\[ E_{\gamma}^{\mathrm{VAL}} = \sum_{n=1}^{N_{\mathrm{VAL}}} \big(t_n - g(x_n|\vb{w})\big)^2 \]

Choose the \(\gamma^*\) with the best (lowest) validation error, put all the data back together (training plus validation, \(N\) samples) and minimise

\[ \begin{aligned} E_{\gamma^*} = {} & \sum_{n=1}^{N} \big(t_n - g(x_n|\vb{w})\big)^2 \\ & + \gamma^* \sum_{q=1}^{Q} w_q^2 \end{aligned} \]

The performance of the final model is reported on the test set.

The penalty is a training device, not a measure of prediction quality, so it is not part of the validation error. The training error cannot be used for the selection because it is non-decreasing in \(\gamma\): it would always select \(\gamma = 0\), i.e. no regularisation.

Формулы и порядок шагов — со слайда 16 лекции 3. Балл легко потерять в двух местах: оставляют штраф в validation error (как в конспекте — это ошибка) и забывают про повторное обучение на объединённых данных. Фраза про test set показывает, что ты различаешь выбор модели и её оценку.

Урок 22 · Пружины на весах · вопрос 5 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following statements about L2 weight decay are correct? Select all that apply.

  • It corresponds to MAP estimation with a zero-mean Gaussian prior on the weights. Минус логарифм гауссианы — квадрат, отсюда штраф \(\gamma \sum_q w_q^2\).
  • It reduces the number of parameters of the network. Число весов то же; уменьшается эффективная ёмкость, а не номинальная.
  • A smaller prior variance \(\sigma_w^2\) corresponds to a larger \(\gamma\). \(\gamma = \sigma^2 / \sigma_w^2\): узкий prior — жёсткая пружина.
  • It sets many weights exactly to zero. Это свойство L1. L2 сжимает пропорционально и до нуля не доводит.
  • If the regularised error is minimised exactly, increasing \(\gamma\) cannot decrease the sum of squared errors on the training set. Больший штраф сильнее отвлекает от подгонки: бирюзовая кривая в лаборатории только растёт.
  • \(\gamma\) can be learned by gradient descent together with the weights, by minimising the training error. На training set оптимум всегда \(\gamma = 0\); её выбирают по validation set.
Show model answer

Correct: the first, the third and the fifth. Weight decay is MAP with a Gaussian prior \(N(0, \sigma_w^2)\) and \(\gamma = \sigma^2 / \sigma_w^2\); it keeps all the weights but shrinks them, so the fit to the training data can only get worse as \(\gamma\) grows. Exact zeros are produced by L1, and \(\gamma\) is a hyperparameter chosen on validation data.

В вопросах «select all» каждое утверждение проверяется отдельно. Типичная ошибка — отметить «exactly to zero»: слово exactly превращает верную мысль «веса уменьшаются» в неверную.

Урок 22 · Пружины на весах · вопрос 6 из 6 · открыть в уроке

A model with a single weight: \(t_n = w + \epsilon_n\), \(\epsilon_n \sim N(0, \sigma^2)\) with \(\sigma^2 = 4\). There are \(N = 4\) observations with sample mean 2. The prior is \(w \sim N(0, \sigma_w^2)\) with \(\sigma_w^2 = 2.25\). Compute \(w_{\mathrm{MAP}}\).

Show model answer

\(\gamma = \sigma^2 / \sigma_w^2 = 4 / 2.25 = 1.778\). Minimising \(\sum_n (t_n - w)^2 + \gamma w^2\):

\[ -2 \sum_n (t_n - w) + 2\gamma w = 0 \] \[ w_{\mathrm{MAP}} = \frac{\sum_n t_n}{N + \gamma} = \frac{8}{4 + 1.778} = 1.385 \]

The maximum likelihood estimate is the sample mean, 2; the prior pulls it towards zero.

Проверяют, что ты умеешь довести MAP до числа: сначала \(\gamma\) как отношение дисперсий, потом производная. Быстрая самопроверка: ответ обязан лежать между 0 и \(w_{\mathrm{MLE}} = 2\).

Урок 23 · Тренировка с отсутствующими

Урок 23 · Тренировка с отсутствующими · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Describe dropout at training time and at test time. Why is a scaling factor needed? State where the factor is applied in (a) the weight scaling rule and (b) inverted dropout.

Show model answer

Training. Let \(p\) be the probability of keeping a unit. At every iteration each hidden unit is kept with probability \(p\) and set to zero with probability \(1 - p\), independently of the other units: \(\tilde h_j = m_j h_j\) with \(m_j \sim Be(p)\). A new mask is sampled every time, so different mini-batches train different thinned networks; the same mask is used in the forward and in the backward pass, so only the weights of the sampled thinned network are updated.

Test. The masks are removed: no unit is dropped. Since \(\E[\tilde h_j] = p\,h_j\), the next layer was trained on inputs that are on average \(p\) times the full ones; with all units active, the weighted sums it receives would be \(1/p\) times larger than their average during training, so its units would work in a regime they were not trained for.

(a) Weight scaling rule: use the full network and multiply the activations of the dropout layer (equivalently, its outgoing weights) by \(p\) at test time; this single pass approximately averages the outputs of all the thinned networks. (b) Inverted dropout: divide the kept activations by \(p\) during training, \(\tilde h_j = m_j h_j / p\), and use the plain network at test time. Both make the expected pre-activation of the next layer the same in training and at test time.

Проверяют три вещи: что маска случайная и новая на каждом шаге; что на тесте работают все нейроны; что множитель назван с местом и значением (\(p\) на тесте или \(1/p\) на обучении). Обязательно скажи словами, что \(p\) — keep probability: на слайде 17 лекции 3 буквой \(p_j^{(l)}\) названа вероятность нейрон обнулить, и без слов твой множитель прочтут наоборот. Слова слайда 22 лекции 3, которые стоит повторить: remove masks, average output, weight scaling.

Урок 23 · Тренировка с отсутствующими · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A hidden layer outputs \(h = [0.4,\ 0.7,\ 0.3,\ 0.1,\ 0.9]\). It was trained with classical dropout, keep probability \(p = 0.6\). A unit of the next layer has all five incoming weights equal to 1 and zero bias. Compute its pre-activation at test time under the weight scaling rule.

Show model answer

At test time no unit is dropped and every activation is multiplied by \(p\):

\[ \begin{aligned} a &= 0.6 \cdot (0.4 + 0.7 + 0.3 + 0.1 + 0.9) \\ &= 0.6 \cdot 2.4 = 1.44 \end{aligned} \]

Две типичные ошибки: применить на тесте маску (на тесте её нет) и умножить на \(1 - p = 0.4\). Ответ 2.4 означает, что поправка забыта.

Урок 23 · Тренировка с отсутствующими · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Same layer and same next-layer unit as above, but now the network is trained with inverted dropout, keep probability \(p = 0.6\). In one training step the sampled mask is \(m = [1,\ 0,\ 1,\ 0,\ 1]\). Compute the pre-activation of the next-layer unit in this step (three decimals).

Show model answer

Kept activations are divided by \(p\), dropped ones are zero:

\[ \tilde h = [0.667,\ 0,\ 0.5,\ 0,\ 1.5] \] \[ a = \frac{0.4 + 0.3 + 0.9}{0.6} = \frac{1.6}{0.6} \approx 2.667 \]

At test time the plain network gives \(a = 2.4\), which is the expected value of \(a\) over all masks.

Проверяют, что ты отличаешь inverted dropout от классического: здесь множитель \(1/p\) стоит на обучении. Ответ 1.6 — это классический вариант, 0.96 — лишнее умножение на \(p\).

Урок 23 · Тренировка с отсутствующими · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A network is trained with dropout: each hidden unit is set to zero with probability 0.3. For a test input a hidden layer outputs \(h = [0.5,\ 0.2,\ 0.8]\). A unit of the next layer has incoming weights \(w = [1,\ {-2},\ 1]\) and bias \(w_0 = 0.1\). Compute its pre-activation at test time under weight scaling.

Show model answer

The probability given is that of dropping a unit, so each unit is kept with probability \(1 - 0.3 = 0.7\). At test time the masks are removed and the weights coming out of the dropout layer are multiplied by the keep probability; the bias is not scaled:

\[ \sum_j w_j h_j = 0.5 - 0.4 + 0.8 = 0.9 \] \[ a = 0.7 \cdot 0.9 + 0.1 = 0.73 \]

Условие написано словами слайда 17 лекции 3: 0.3 — вероятность выключить, а множитель на тесте — вероятность остаться. Ответ 0.37 — умножение на 0.3 вместо 0.7. Ответ 0.70 — множитель попал и на bias. Ответ 1.0 — поправка забыта.

Урок 23 · Тренировка с отсутствующими · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Give two reasons why dropout reduces overfitting. How many thinned networks does a hidden layer with 10 units define? How does dropout differ from bagging?

Show model answer

1. It prevents co-adaptation. By randomly turning off some neurons we force each hidden unit to learn an independent feature: it cannot rely on specific other units being present, so its feature has to be useful together with many random subsets of the other units. The representation becomes redundant and robust.

2. It behaves as an ensemble method. A layer with 10 droppable units defines \(2^{10} = 1024\) thinned networks that share weights. Dropout trains these weaker classifiers on different mini-batches: each training iteration updates one randomly sampled thinned network. At test time the masks are removed and we implicitly average the responses of all ensemble members: weight scaling approximates the average of their predictions, which reduces variance.

Bagging trains a few independent models, each with its own weights, to convergence on bootstrap samples and averages their outputs explicitly. Dropout trains exponentially many weight-sharing sub-networks, each for at most a few steps, and averages them implicitly with a single forward pass.

Ждут оба объяснения и оба ключевых слова: co-adaptation и ensemble (model averaging). Первая причина — это слайд 17 лекции 3 почти дословно (independent feature, rely on other units), вторая — слайд 21 лекции 3 (weaker classifiers, implicitly average, ensemble members). Про bagging достаточно двух отличий: общие веса и неявное усреднение.

Урок 23 · Тренировка с отсутствующими · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A network is trained with dropout on its hidden layers; \(p\) denotes the keep probability. Which statements are true? Select all that apply.

  • At test time all hidden units are active. Маски бросают только на обучении.
  • With classical dropout the outgoing weights of a dropout layer are multiplied by \(p\) at test time. Это weight scaling rule.
  • With inverted dropout the activations are multiplied by \(p\) at test time. В inverted dropout на тесте нет множителей: поправка \(1/p\) уже сделана на обучении.
  • Dropout permanently removes the units with the smallest weights. Это pruning. В dropout маска случайная и новая на каждом шаге, на тесте все нейроны на месте.
  • The incoming and outgoing weights of a unit dropped for a given training example get zero gradient from that example. Его выход равен нулю, ошибка через него назад не проходит.
  • Dropout adds a penalty term to the error function. Штраф добавляет weight decay. Dropout шумит в активациях, формула \(E(w)\) остаётся прежней.
Show model answer

True: the first, the second and the fifth. Dropout is active only in training; classical dropout rescales by \(p\) at test time, inverted dropout rescales by \(1/p\) in training and does nothing at test time; a dropped unit has zero output, hence its weights get zero gradient from that example. Dropout is neither pruning nor a penalty on the error function.

В вопросах «select all» чаще всего ловят на двух подменах: dropout против pruning и множитель \(p\) не в том варианте.

Урок 23 · Тренировка с отсутствующими · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Early stopping, weight decay and dropout all reduce the effective capacity of a network without removing any weight. For each technique state what it constrains and name its hyperparameter. How are these hyperparameters chosen?

Show model answer

Early stopping constrains the number of training iterations, i.e. how far the weights can move from their initial values; hyperparameter: patience. Weight decay constrains the magnitude of the weights through a penalty on \(\sum_q w_q^2\) added to the error function; hyperparameter: \(\gamma\). Dropout constrains how much units depend on each other, by multiplicative noise on the activations (implicit model averaging); hyperparameter: the keep probability \(p\).

All three are selected on validation data (hold-out or cross-validation): never on the training error, which would always choose no regularisation, and never on the test set.

Это вопрос на весь блок. Ценится параллельная структура ответа: «что ограничивает — чем управляется» трижды, и одна общая фраза про validation.

Урок 24 · Испорченный телефон

Урок 24 · Испорченный телефон · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

What is the vanishing gradient problem? Which layers does it affect most, and why do sigmoid and tanh activations cause it? Name three remedies.

Show model answer

In backpropagation the error signal of a hidden unit is built from the signals of the next layer:

\[ \delta_i^{(l-1)} = g'\big(a_i^{(l-1)}\big) \sum_j w_{ji}^{(l)}\, \delta_j^{(l)} \]

Unrolling this recursion, the gradient that reaches the first layer is a product of \(L-1\) per-layer factors of size about \(\abs{w \cdot g'(a)}\). For the sigmoid \(g' \le 0.25\) (and \(g' \approx 0\) when the unit is saturated); for tanh \(g' \le 1\) (and \(\approx 0\) when saturated). With factors below 1 the product shrinks exponentially with depth, so the layers closest to the input receive almost no gradient and their weights are not updated, although the error is still large.

For example, the gradient of a first-layer weight in a network with one hidden layer, \(\partial E / \partial w_{ji}^{(1)}\), already contains two activation derivatives, \(g_1'\) and \(h_j'\). Sigmoid and tanh saturate, so this derivative gets close to zero and is never above 1; backpropagation multiplies such derivatives, hence the gradient far away from the output vanishes and learning in a deep network does not happen. The problem is best known from recurrent neural networks, but it affects every deep network.

Remedies: non-saturating activations such as ReLU (\(g' = 1\) for \(a \gt 0\)); variance-preserving weight initialisation (Xavier, He); batch normalisation; residual connections.

Проверяют три вещи: механизм (произведение множителей по слоям, а не одно слово saturation), кто страдает (слои у входа) и конкретные лекарства. Сильный ответ отдельно говорит, что у sigmoid затухание есть и без насыщения, потому что \(g' \le 0.25\). Скелет ответа — четыре пункта профессора (лекция 3, слайд 24): по ним написан второй абзац ответа.

Урок 24 · Испорченный телефон · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A chain of 10 sigmoid neurons (one neuron per layer) has all weights equal to 1. Compute the largest possible value of \(\abs{\delta^{(1)}} / \abs{\delta^{(10)}}\), i.e. the largest fraction of the error signal that can reach the first neuron. Give the answer in units of \(10^{-6}\) (type 2.5 for \(2.5 \cdot 10^{-6}\)). What changes if the neurons are ReLU?

Show model answer

Going from neuron 10 back to neuron 1 takes 9 backward steps. Each step multiplies the signal by \(w \cdot g'(a) \le 1 \cdot 0.25\), with equality only at \(a = 0\):

\[ \frac{\abs{\delta^{(1)}}}{\abs{\delta^{(10)}}} \le 0.25^{9} \approx 3.81 \cdot 10^{-6} \]

With ReLU and active units (\(a \gt 0\)) each factor is exactly \(w \cdot 1 = 1\): no attenuation. Through any inactive unit the factor is exactly 0.

Частая ошибка — возвести в десятую степень: нейронов 10, а передач между ними 9. Вторая — забыть слово «at most»: это верхняя граница, она достигается только при \(a = 0\) во всех нейронах.

Урок 24 · Испорченный телефон · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A hidden unit \(i\) has pre-activation \(a_i = -2\). The backpropagated sum from the next layer is \(\sum_j w_{ji}\,\delta_j = 0.5\). Compute the error signal \(\delta_i\) of the unit if its activation is (a) ReLU, (b) Leaky ReLU with \(\alpha = 0.01\), (c) ELU with \(\alpha = 1\). Enter the value for (c).

Show model answer

\(\delta_i = g'(a_i) \cdot \sum_j w_{ji}\,\delta_j\), with the derivative taken on the branch \(a \le 0\).

(a) ReLU: \(g'(-2) = 0\), so \(\delta_i = 0\).

(b) Leaky ReLU: \(g'(-2) = \alpha = 0.01\), so \(\delta_i = 0.01 \cdot 0.5 = 0.005\).

(c) ELU: \(g'(-2) = \alpha e^{-2} = 0.1353\), so \(\delta_i = 0.1353 \cdot 0.5 = 0.0677\).

Проверяют производные слева от нуля. Ровный ноль у ReLU — причина dying ReLU; у Leaky ReLU и ELU сигнал остаётся. Для ELU удобно помнить \(g'(a) = g(a) + \alpha\): здесь \(-0.8647 + 1 = 0.1353\).

Урок 24 · Испорченный телефон · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Define the ReLU activation function and its derivative. List its advantages and disadvantages. What is a dying ReLU, and how do Leaky ReLU and ELU address it?

Show model answer

\(g(a) = \max(0, a)\); \(g'(a) = 1_{a \gt 0}\), i.e. 1 for \(a \gt 0\) and 0 for \(a \lt 0\) (not differentiable at 0; by convention \(g'(0) = 0\)).

Advantages: faster SGD convergence (about 6 times faster than tanh in Krizhevsky et al., 2012); sparse activation (only part of the hidden units are active); efficient gradient propagation: it does not saturate for \(a \gt 0\), so the backward factor \(g'\) of an active unit is exactly 1 and the activation itself neither shrinks nor amplifies the gradient (the weights still can); efficient computation (just a threshold at zero, no exponential). It is also scale-invariant: \(\max(0, c\,a) = c \max(0, a)\) for \(c \ge 0\).

Disadvantages: not differentiable at zero (but differentiable anywhere else); output not zero-centred; unbounded output, which could potentially blow up; dying neurons.

A ReLU neuron dies when it is pushed into a state in which it is inactive for essentially all inputs: its pre-activation is negative for all training samples. It happens with high learning rates (one too large update), and also with a large negative bias or after a poor initialisation. Its output is 0 and \(g' = 0\), so no gradient flows backward through the neuron: its \(\delta\) and the gradients of its weights and bias are exactly zero, it is stuck and is never updated again. Many dead neurons mean a decreased model capacity. Leaky ReLU fixes the dying ReLU problem with a small slope on the left: \(g(a) = 0.01\,a\) for \(a \lt 0\) (in general \(\alpha a\)), so the gradient there is \(\alpha\) instead of 0. ELU uses \(g(a) = \alpha(e^{a} - 1)\) for \(a \lt 0\), with gradient \(\alpha e^{a} \gt 0\); it also makes the mean activation closer to zero, which speeds up learning, and its \(\alpha\) is tuned by hand.

Балл теряют на двух местах. Первое: в определении мёртвого нейрона нужны слова «for all training samples» (на слайде 27 лекции 3 — "for essentially all inputs"): нейрон, выдавший ноль на одном примере, не мёртв. Второе: в минусах пишут только dying ReLU и забывают три остальных.

Списки плюсов и минусов в ответе — почти дословно со слайдов (лекция 3, слайд 26 и слайд 27), формулы Leaky ReLU и ELU — со слайда 28 лекции 3. Только плюс efficient gradient propagation записан точнее слайда: с оговоркой про множитель \(g'\) и веса.

Урок 24 · Испорченный телефон · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following are advantages of the ReLU activation function with respect to sigmoid and tanh, as seen in class? Select all that apply.

  • Faster convergence of SGD. Первый плюс слайда 26 лекции 3; там же число — "6x w.r.t sigmoid/tanh".
  • Zero-centred output. Выход ReLU всегда \(\ge 0\). На слайде 27 лекции 3 это минус: "Non-zero centered output".
  • Sparse activation: only part of the hidden units are active. Второй плюс слайда 26 лекции 3. Не путай с мёртвыми нейронами: те молчат на всех входах.
  • Efficient gradient propagation: an active unit passes the backpropagated signal with derivative exactly 1. Третий плюс слайда 26 лекции 3. Точная версия — именно про множитель \(g'\) активного нейрона; веса остаются в произведении.
  • Bounded output, so that activations cannot blow up. Всё наоборот. На слайде 27 лекции 3 среди минусов: "unbounded value (could potentially blow up)".
  • Efficient computation: just thresholding at zero. Четвёртый плюс слайда 26 лекции 3: сравнение с нулём вместо экспоненты.
  • Differentiability everywhere. В нуле излом. На слайде 27 лекции 3 это первый минус: "Non-differentiable at zero".
Show model answer

Correct: faster SGD convergence, sparse activation, efficient gradient propagation and efficient computation. These are the four advantages listed in class. The other three statements are the opposite of ReLU's potential disadvantages: its output is not zero-centred, it is unbounded, and it is not differentiable at zero. The fourth disadvantage is dying neurons.

Вопрос на узнавание двух списков профессора: четыре плюса (лекция 3, слайд 26) и четыре минуса (лекция 3, слайд 27). Если в тесте плюс про градиент записан словами слайда, "no vanishing or exploding gradient problems", это тот же третий плюс; ложным его делают добавки вроде "whatever the weights are", как в следующем вопросе.

Урок 24 · Испорченный телефон · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Select all correct statements.

  • With sigmoid activations the gradient can vanish even if no neuron is saturated. \(g' \le 0.25\) всегда, и произведение таких множителей убывает экспоненциально.
  • ReLU removes both vanishing and exploding gradients, whatever the weights are. Множитель на слой равен \(w \cdot g'(a)\). ReLU делает \(g' = 1\), но веса остаются в произведении.
  • A dead ReLU neuron receives zero gradient for its weights and bias on every training sample. \(\delta_j = g'(a_j) \cdot (\dots) = 0\) на каждом примере.
  • For a sigmoid unit, a per-layer factor \(\abs{w\,g'(a)} \gt 1\) requires \(\abs{w} \gt 4\). \(g' \le 1/4\), значит нужно \(\abs{w} \gt 4\) даже в лучшей точке \(a = 0\).
  • Leaky ReLU has derivative \(\alpha\) for \(a \gt 0\). Наоборот: \(\alpha\) слева, при \(a \le 0\); справа производная равна 1.
  • tanh does not saturate because its output is zero-centred. Центрирование и насыщение — разные свойства. Tanh насыщается, причём быстрее sigmoid.
Show model answer

Correct: the first, third and fourth statements. The per-layer backward factor is \(w \cdot g'(a)\): ReLU fixes \(g'\) on the active side but not \(w\); the sigmoid derivative is bounded by 1/4; a dead ReLU has \(g' = 0\) on all samples; Leaky ReLU's slope \(\alpha\) applies to \(a \le 0\); tanh saturates for large \(\abs{a}\).

Каждая строка проверяет один факт о множителе \(w \cdot g'(a)\). Ловушка — второе утверждение. Фраза «ReLU solves the vanishing gradient» встречается в учебниках, а среди плюсов ReLU на слайде стоит то же обещание (лекция 3, слайд 26). Без оговорки про веса это неверно.

Урок 24 · Испорченный телефон · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Suppose every layer multiplies the backpropagated gradient by a factor of 2. What is the minimum number of layers after which the gradient has been amplified by more than a factor of 1000? Which symptoms would you observe during training, and what is a standard remedy?

Show model answer

We need \(2^{n} \gt 1000\), i.e. \(n \gt \log_2 1000 \approx 9.97\), so \(n = 10\) (\(2^{10} = 1024\)). With 50 layers the factor is \(2^{50} \approx 1.13 \cdot 10^{15}\).

This is the exploding gradient problem: when the weights are too large, the gradient grows as it passes back through each layer. Symptoms: the error (loss) suddenly becomes NaN or Inf; the weights take extremely large values; the gradient norm is in the hundreds or thousands; training oscillates wildly or diverges. Remedies: gradient clipping; proper weight initialisation (Xavier, He); normalisation layers; LSTM or GRU units in recurrent networks.

Числовая часть простая; баллы дают за связку «множитель на слой больше 1 → экспоненциальный рост → симптомы → gradient clipping».

Урок 25 · Ксерокопия ксерокопии

Урок 25 · Ксерокопия ксерокопии · вопрос 1 из 8 · открыть в уроке

Derive the Xavier initialization as seen in class. Start from a linear neuron with \(I\) inputs and random weights, state your assumptions and obtain the condition that keeps the variance of the output equal to the variance of the input. Then give the analogous condition for the gradient and the final formula of Glorot & Bengio.

Show model answer

Consider a linear neuron with \(I\) inputs \(x_1, \dots, x_I\) and random weights; the bias is initialized to zero. Its output is

\[ h_j = w_{j1}x_1 + \dots + w_{jI}x_I \]

The weights are drawn independently of the inputs, so the variance of one term is

\[ \begin{aligned} \mathrm{Var}(w_{ji}x_i) &= \E[x_i]^2\,\mathrm{Var}(w_{ji}) \\ &\quad {} + \E[w_{ji}]^2\,\mathrm{Var}(x_i) \\ &\quad {} + \mathrm{Var}(w_{ji})\,\mathrm{Var}(x_i) \end{aligned} \]

With zero-mean inputs and zero-mean weights the first two terms vanish:
